  - D9 — O-Lyrica-verbatim NE port would be a BUG here (new risk #43): O-Contrabass's notePitchbendChanged recomputes currentFrequency from cached tuningEngineBaseFreqHz (Q17), so applyPendingTuning must fold into the CACHE at noteStarted, else the first mid-note bend silently drops the Dorico offset.   Phase 2.5 research complete 2026-04-30. RESEARCH.md §21 Phase 2.5 Body Resonator + Bow Noise Generator appended (~684 LOC; total file 7367 LOC). 12 sub-sections §21.1–§21.18 resolve all 14 CONTEXT rev-10 Open Q1–Q14.   §21.1 Pre-Phase-2.5 repro tripwire EXECUTED PASS — 13/13 goldens reproduce byte-identical at HEAD descendant of `1dfca9d`; tripwire CLEARED. §21.2 + §21.3 surface **TWO CRITICAL ESCALATIONS for plan-phase**:

  **ESCALATION-1 (§21.2): O-Bowed verbatim-copy assumption BROKEN.** O-Bowed `Source/DSP/BodyResonator.{h,cpp}` (62+188 LOC) is a 4-preset MORPHABLE design (Membrane/Wood/Metal/Glass) using `makePeakFilter`+stereo+processor-level integration with `setMaterial/setSize/setBodyAmount` API and cross-preset `normGain`. ARCHITECTURE-spec'd O-Contrabass BodyResonator is a SINGLE-PRESET bass-tuned design using `makeBandPass`+mono+voice-level integration with `setSize/setDamping/setMix` API, 35 Hz HP one-pole on dry path, no normGain, per-block coefficient recompute (vs O-Bowed lazy-on-change). Twelve discrete deltas tabulated in §21.2.2. O-Bowed `BowNoiseGenerator.h` (54 LOC) is SINGLE-BPF continuous-noise (3464 Hz, Q=0.87, voice-index seeded `voiceIndex * 31337` deterministic) with no slip-burst mechanism; ARCHITECTURE-spec'd O-Contrabass version is 3-BAND BPF (700/1500/3000 Hz, Q≈1.0/1.2/1.5) + per-period exponential-decay slip bursts + bowEnergy envelope `clamp(0,1, |v_bow|·F_bow / (v_ref·F_ref))`. Six discrete deltas tabulated in §21.2.4. **LOC budget revised: ~410–590 LOC NEW across 3 source files** (vs CONTEXT rev-10 §"Open Q #2" estimate ~200–500). Plan-phase action: acknowledge CONTEXT Q54 deviation in PLAN rev-12 §"Approach Decisions" with explicit deviation flag; do NOT re-discuss (research-phase finding is design-grounded, not opinion-grounded). Recommended class designs locked in §21.2.6 (BodyResonator) + §21.2.7 (BowNoiseGenerator) as plan-phase starting code.

  **ESCALATION-2 (§21.3): No Helmholtz slip-detection accessor available within CONTEXT 4-file scope.** `modules/synthesis/bow-friction/cpp/HyperbolicFriction.h` is explicitly memoryless (`computeReflectionCoefficient(v_delta, F_bow)` returns rho only; no F_friction history, no stick/slip phase tracking). `BowModel.h` provides only envelope outputs. `WaveguideString.cpp:190` invokes friction once per sample but exposes no friction-force accessor. True ARCHITECTURE §165 "zero-crossing of friction force from stick to slip" requires WaveguideString.{h,cpp} edit which violates CONTEXT line 156 4-file scope-strict rule. **Period-heuristic v1.0 substitute LOCKED**: voice tracks `f0_active` from active-string fundamental, pushes via `setFundamentalHz(f0)`, BowNoiseGenerator decrements per-sample `slipCounter` and fires burst (envelope = bowEnergy at trigger, decay 0.999 per sample → ~70 ms decay at 48 kHz) every `sr/f0` samples. Trade-offs: ✅ within 4-file scope, ✅ bit-deterministic integer counter math, ✅ matches ARCHITECTURE §165 decay spec audibly; ❌ burst phase NOT locked to actual Helmholtz stick→slip transition. Three scope-expansion alternatives deferred (Option A voice-level F_friction reconstruction, Option B WaveguideString getLastFrictionForce() accessor, Option C WaveguideString slip-flag accessor) → Phase 2.5-bis or v1.1 backlog.

  **ESCALATION-3 (§21.16): ARCHITECTURE.md §149 vs §509 size_scalar inconsistency.** §149 commentary claims "1.83:1 frequency span across knob" with formula `size_scalar = 0.85 + 0.30·s`; computed range is 1.353:1 (0.85 → 1.15), NOT 1.83:1. Plan-phase decision: lock formula §509 verbatim (formula authoritative over commentary). Mode 1 fc range: 52.17 Hz (s=1.0) ↔ 70.59 Hz (s=0.0). Append to deferred ARCHITECTURE.md amendment list at end-of-Stage-2 verify (post-Phase-2.6) alongside §"DC Blocker" + §"In-loop saturator".

  **Resolved-via-pattern-inheritance:** §21.6 `juce::Random` seed determinism via O-Bowed `voiceIndex * 31337` constructor-time fixed pattern (deterministic per voice; O-Contrabass monophonic confirmed at PluginProcessor.cpp:129 — voiceIndex=0). §21.7 body-coefficient stability at low-freq edge analytically STABLE: Mode 1 worst-case at fc=52.17 Hz, Q=14, sr=44.1 kHz → pole radius `r = exp(-π·52/(14·44100)) ≈ 0.9997` (3 ‱ inside unit circle); float32 eps=1.19e-7 well-resolves; `juce::ScopedNoDenormals` already in voice render path. Mode 8 worst-case at fc=1411 Hz, Q=0.375 → wide-bandwidth filter, trivially stable. §21.13 CMakeLists.txt source-list addition required for `Source/DSP/BodyResonator.cpp` (folded into R37a task).

phase_2_3_research_anomaly_schelleng_clamp: |
  §16.3 closed-form Schelleng wedge produces negative headroom at default
  bass operating point (BOW_SPEED=0.15, BOW_PRESSURE=1.0, β=0.10): hUp=+0.60,
  hLo=−0.84 → headroom=−0.84. Same Risk #7 pattern as Phase 2.1c E1 dispersion
  clamp. v1.0 implements formula AS-WRITTEN (slow-LFO silenced at default bass);
  Phase 2.4 calibration polynomial parked. HR-4 hard rule skips wedge math at
  SLOW_LFO_DEPTH=0 to preserve bit-exact regression.
phase_2_1c_research_anomaly_e1_clamp: |
  Closed-form -C/k ≈ 15 across the entire B envelope at I=8.0 (E1) → clamps
  to a≈+0.99 regardless of STRING_STIFFNESS. Paper validity envelope is
  piano register; bass register sits outside. NOT a bug; audible sweep may
  be flatter than ideal. Phase 2.4 follow-up if R18 reveals the sweep is
  musically uninteresting (calibration polynomial for bass register). Gate
  3 stability + bit-exact regression bar still meaningful.

## Completed So Far

**Ideation:** Complete
- Core concept defined: bass-only specialized 4-string contrabass physical model, sustained orchestral arco + ambient drone
- Differentiation from O-Bowed clarified (deep specialization vs general-purpose)
- Parameters specified across 8 sections (Bow / Body / Strings / Detune / Expression / Drone / Output / Microtonal) — 29 total
- Sonic targets locked: deep wood body resonance, bow noise / rosin grit, slow expressive attack
- Drone-first features designed in (infinite sustain, sub-harmonics, slow-bow LFO, per-string detuning)
- Layered expression model defined (intrinsic CC + dedicated vibrato + Expression Macro)
- Full Ouaricon microtonal convention (Note Expression + MTS-ESP + Scala/TUN + MPE)
- Requirements extracted: 14 must / 7 should / 3 nice across FUNC, DSP, UI, PERF, COMPAT, QUAL

**Stage 0:** Complete (Research & Planning)
- Synthesized 4 deep-research documents (synthesis + waveguide-stability + body-acoustics + drone-and-subharmonics) into canonical architecture
- ARCHITECTURE.md documented with 11 required sections (immutable contract for Stages 1-4)
- All 29 parameters mapped to DSP components (no orphans, no unmapped components)
- 9 distinct DSP components specified with JUCE class assignments
- Integration analysis: feature dependencies, parameter interactions, processing order, thread boundaries
- 5 high-risk components documented with fallback architectures
- 6 architecture decisions recorded with rationale and alternatives
- Complexity score: 5.0 (capped from raw 16.0) — Tier 6 (Deep)
- Implementation strategy: phased across all stages with 6 DSP sub-phases
- 6 open decisions resolved with recommendations and defer-to-v1.1 flags
- ROADMAP.md documents Stage 1 (Foundation) → Stage 2 (DSP, 6 phases) → Stage 3 (GUI, 3 phases) → Stage 4 (Polish)
- Estimated total effort: 10–15 days

## Context to Preserve

**Architecture highlights:**
- 4-string EADG digital waveguide (E1-G3) with `juce::dsp::DelayLine<float, Lagrange3rd>` (8192-sample buffer)
- Hyperbolic friction junction at 2x oversampling (`filterHalfBandPolyphaseIIR`)
- Cascaded allpass dispersion per string (M=4/3/2/1 for E/A/D/G)
- 8-mode parallel biquad body bank (Askenfelt-derived, Body Size scales freq, Q invariant)
- 3-band BPF bow noise (700/1500/3000 Hz) summed AFTER body resonator
- Drone features: Infinite Sustain (loop gain 0.997 → 0.99995), Sub-Harmonics (period-doubling friction bias), Slow-Bow LFO (Schelleng-aware diagonal modulation)
- Vibrato modulates delay-line length (physically correct; Lagrange3rd absorbs cleanly)
- Master saturator (polynomial) + zero-latency feedforward limiter (-1 dBFS)
- Ouaricon microtonal: priority Note Expression > MTS-ESP > Scala/TUN > MPE pitch-bend > 12-TET

**Module dependencies:**
- `modules/tuning/scala-tuning-engine` v2.1.0 (existing) — Scala/TUN + MTS-ESP
- `modules/tuning/note-expression` (existing) — VST3 Note Expression helper + JUCE patch
- `modules/dsp/bow-friction` (TO BE CREATED in Phase 2.1b) — extracted from O-Bowed `HyperbolicFriction.h`

**Reference plugins:**
- O-Bowed — friction junction, waveguide string, body resonator (extraction source)
- O-Lyrica — Note Expression integration pattern (BowedStringVoice template)

**Performance projection:** ~3.2% CPU on M1 (well under PERF-02's 5% target)

**Highest-risk component:** Friction Junction at E1 + max INFINITE_SUSTAIN + max SUB_HARMONICS — represents ~50% of project risk. Phase 2.1 must validate stability before any further features added.

**Key constraints (from juce8-critical-patterns + memory file):**
- `IS_SYNTH TRUE` + `NEEDS_MIDI_INPUT TRUE` mandatory
- `BusesProperties` output-only in constructor
- `JUCE-NE-PATCH` must be applied to `~/JUCE/` for Note Expression
- `getLatencySamples()` is NOT virtual in JUCE 8 — use `setLatencySamples()` in `prepareToPlay`
- WebView2 needs both `NEEDS_WEBVIEW2 TRUE` AND `JUCE_USE_WIN_WEBVIEW2_WITH_STATIC_LINKING=1`
- Resource provider receives PATHS, not full URLs

**Open decisions resolved (see ROADMAP.md §"Open Decisions Resolved"):**
1. Friction tier: Hyperbolic only in v1.0 (defer elasto-plastic to v1.1)
2. Module extraction: DURING Stage 2 (mid-Phase 2.1)
3. Authentic Arco wolf toggle: defer to v1.1
4. Sub-harmonic max depth: 1 octave (f0/2)
5. Body Size mapping: full 1/4 → 4/4 span
6. Wood variants: single fixed wood for v1.0 (defer 2-variant to v1.1)

Six §10/SUMMARY hypotheses dispositioned: H1 (sign convention) disproven (round-trip closure correct), H3 (saturator) disproven (linear at −32.6 dBFS), H4 (BowModel sr) disproven (`prepare(spec_at_2x.sampleRate)` correct), H6 (first-tick envelope) dissolved (envelope behaves correctly; transient peak is sticking-injection + DCB transient, not envelope timing), H2 (DCB) reframed → B3, H5 (Schelleng) reframed → B1 + latent for Phase 2.4. PLAN rev-3 deliverable: F1 split-rail (~+45 LOC, formerly §10 R1), F2 bridge LP recurrence fix (1-line: drop `g` from feedback), F3 remove in-loop DCB (~−6 LOC, ARCH.md §"DC Blocker" deviation justified per §11.6 — once F2 fixes LP DC gain to `g`, in-loop DCB is redundant and harmful), F4 drop `betaScale` fudge in voice (~−2 LOC, formerly §10 R5). Verification: V1 render-harness rerun (Gate 1: pass_rms must transition from false → true; expected `rmsMid_s5_s6` ≈ 0.05–0.20), V3 auval + pluginval-10 (Gate 2). V2 instrumentation hook (per-sample `v_delta / frictionVelocity` CSV trace) optional, only if V1 fails. Hand off to `/plugin-plan O-Contrabass 2-dsp` for PLAN rev-3.

Q7a default flip is provably zero-delta until macro DSP wires. **§16.2 (Q1):** vibrato S-curve = half-cosine `0.5−0.5·cos(π·t/0.3)` for `t∈[0, 0.3]` (architecture-verbatim); per-sample `cosf` cost trivial inside 300 ms onset window; **note-off 150 ms LINEAR fade-out** (different from onset envelope; captures `vibratoOnsetGateAtNoteOff` at fade entry to avoid discontinuity on fast note-on→note-off mid-onset). **§16.3 (Q2) — Schelleng wedge bass-register pre-flight (Risk #7-style):** at default bass operating point (BOW_SPEED=0.15, BOW_PRESSURE=1.0, β=0.10, INFINITE_SUSTAIN=0.5, μ_s=0.85, μ_d=0.25, R_s=0.5), closed-form `fMax = (2·Z·v_b)/(β·Δμ) = 2.500`, `fMin = (Z²·v_b)/(2·R·β²·Δμ) = 6.250` (with Z=R=R_s=0.5 dimensionless collapse); `headroomUpper = (2.5−1.0)/2.5 = +0.60`, `headroomLower = (1.0−6.25)/6.25 = −0.84` → `headroom = min(+0.60, −0.84) = −0.84` NEGATIVE. v1.0 ships clamp-engaged-at-default (slow-LFO silenced at default bass settings); user must dial bow params toward mid-wedge to hear LFO modulation. **Phase 2.4 calibration polynomial parked** (analogous to Phase 2.1c E1 dispersion clamp Risk #7). HR-4 hard rule skips wedge math at SLOW_LFO_DEPTH=0 to preserve bit-exact regression bar. `--schelleng-stress` Gate 5 invariant 4 validates clamp-engages on stress preset (BOW_PRESSURE=7.0+BOW_SPEED=0.05): headroomUpper=−7.40, headroomLower=+2.36 → headroom=−7.40 → safeDepth=0 → clampedDepthMean ≈ 0 (instrumentation hook reports). **§16.4 (Q3):** vibrato + detune stacking — combine cents first (`totalCents = detuneCents + vibratoCents`) then single 2^ via `expf(vibCents · −ln2/1200)` (~12 ns vs 25 ns for `pow(2, ...)`); active string only; HR-1 literal-zero short-circuit when `effectiveVibratoDepth = 0`. Lagrange3rd absorbs ±50¢ peak (200× smaller than Phase 2.2 detune-sweep ±1200¢ already validated). **§16.5 (Q4):** brightness 20 ms voice-level `SmoothedValue<Linear>` is sufficient — Δp ≈ 0.015/block at 290 Hz/block macro step (0→500 Hz over 20 ms ≈ 1.72 host blocks); well below ~0.05 zipper threshold for one-pole sweeps. Single voice-level `macroSmoothed` feeds 4 destinations via per-block formula. WaveguideString surface untouched. Fallback to 50 ms (architecture line 522 body-bank precedent) reserved if Gate 5 invariant 5 fails. **§16.6 (Q5):** 7-step per-block evaluation order locked with full pseudocode. Vibrato + slow-LFO phase counters are voice-level (single phase counter each, NOT per-string); they advance regardless of crossfade state. Active-string-only modulation contract (Q2) means vibrato modulates ONLY the new active string's `setDelaySamples` during crossfade; old string's delay held at value set by `noteStarted`. **§16.7 (Q6):** 4 harness modes (`--vibrato`, `--slow-lfo`, `--schelleng-stress`, `--macro-sweep`) specified with full JSON schemas + pass-conditions + per-mode APVTS overrides. **Pitch-tracking via autocorrelation** (NOT FFT bin-shift; bass register at 41.20 Hz needs sub-bin resolution — at sr=44100, 4096-sample FFT bin width ≈10.8 Hz = ~25¢ resolution which is too coarse for ±12¢ vibrato measurement); 4096-sample window with 256-sample hop, parabolic interpolation around peak lag in τ ∈ [400, 1500] samples (29-110 Hz range). `clampedDepthMean` instrumentation hook (atomic `lastSafeDepth` written per-block in step 3, harness-side mean accumulator). **§16.8 (Q7):** bit-exact preservation analytical proof — HR-1 to HR-4 short-circuits + IEEE 754 identity arithmetic (`x + 0.0` and `x · 1.0` bit-identical for all finite x) preserve regression bar through full Phase 2.3 source edits. SmoothedValue<Linear> with `setCurrentAndTargetValue(0.0f)` init has currentValue == targetValue, every getNextValue() returns 0.0f exactly. **§16.9 (Q8):** macro/wedge interaction — NO re-eval against macro-lifted params. Macro is performance-lift, not safety-lift; downstream defenses (algebraic saturator x/√(1+x²), energy clamp, loop-gain ceiling 0.9999999) catch instability from macro-lifted bow params. Verification path = R32 listening test only. **§16.10 (Q9):** `vibratoOnsetTimerSeconds = 0` in `prepareToPlay`; per-note semantics; first note hears configured onset delay. **§16.11 (Q10):** Stage-1 contract amendment grep audit — only `parameter-spec.md` line 57 (default `0.50` → `0.0`) and `STATUS.md` `contract_checksums.parameter_spec` (sha256 update) require edits in R33 atomic commit.
